title: Full changelog for Home Assistant 2026.8
description: Detailed changelog for the Home Assistant 2026.8 release
replace_regex: \s\(\[?[a-z0-9\-\s_]+\]?\)$

These are all the changes included in the Home Assistant 2026.8 release.
For a summary in a more readable format
[Release notes blog for this release](/blog/2026/08/05/release-20268/).

## Beta Fixes

- daikin: map Empty values exception to cannot_connect in config flow ([@Rodrigorm33] - [#174243]) ([daikin docs]) (beta fix)
- Correct description of "shopping_list.complete_item" action ([@jamesmyatt] - [#175534]) ([shopping_list docs]) (beta fix)
- Catch missing restore keys for template cover, device_tracker, and fan ([@Petro31] - [#176724]) ([template docs]) (beta fix)
- Fix CI ([@joostlek] - [#177557]) ([todoist docs]) ([unifiprotect docs]) (beta fix)
- Fix http yaml repair ha version ([@edenhaus] - [#177566]) ([http docs]) (beta fix)
- Bump idasen-ha to 3.0.1 ([@abmantis] - [#177567]) ([idasen_desk docs]) (beta fix)
- Do not set a device on YAML history_stats entities ([@arturpragacz] - [#177594]) ([history_stats docs]) (beta fix)
- Do not set a device on YAML derivative entities ([@arturpragacz] - [#177595]) ([derivative docs]) (beta fix)
- Do not set a device on YAML integration entities ([@arturpragacz] - [#177596]) ([integration docs]) (beta fix)
- Do not set a device on YAML statistics entities ([@arturpragacz] - [#177597]) ([statistics docs]) (beta fix)
- Do not set a device on YAML threshold entities ([@arturpragacz] - [#177598]) ([threshold docs]) (beta fix)
- Do not set a device on YAML trend entities ([@arturpragacz] - [#177599]) ([trend docs]) (beta fix)
- Do not set a device on YAML mold_indicator entities ([@arturpragacz] - [#177600]) ([mold_indicator docs]) (beta fix)
- Do not set a device on YAML generic_hygrostat entities ([@arturpragacz] - [#177601]) ([generic_hygrostat docs]) (beta fix)
- Do not set a device on YAML generic_thermostat entities ([@arturpragacz] - [#177602]) ([generic_thermostat docs]) (beta fix)
- Do not set a device on YAML utility_meter entities ([@arturpragacz] - [#177603]) ([utility_meter docs]) (beta fix)
- Require UniFi Protect 7.1 ([@RaHehl] - [#177620]) ([unifiprotect docs]) (beta fix)
- Do not stage a pending HTTP config when there is no YAML to migrate ([@agners] - [#177622]) ([http docs]) (beta fix)
- Update frontend to 20260729.1 ([@bramkragten] - [#177628]) ([frontend docs]) (beta fix)
- Fix Cookidoo migration duplicate unique IDs ([@Rodrigorm33] - [#174168]) ([cookidoo docs]) (beta fix)
- casper_glow: reject non-Casper Glow Bluetooth discoveries before config-flow handshake ([@Rodrigorm33] - [#174242]) ([casper_glow docs]) (beta fix)

- Bump deebot-client to 18.5.0 ([@edenhaus] - [#177626]) ([ecovacs docs]) (beta fix)
